   188	          >
   189	            In Lighthouse Point, the lot profile — point, deepwater finger, canal-wide, or lakefront — predicts ocean access time and dockage tolerance more reliably than the listing photographs ever do. Buyers paying a waterfront premium should price the lot, not the staging.
   190	          </aside>
   191	
   192	          <p className="mt-7 text-[15px] leading-relaxed text-navy-800/85">
   193	            Lighthouse Point is a small Broward community sold to vessel owners. The marketing language — "boater's paradise," "no-fixed-bridge access," "Hillsboro Inlet to the Atlantic" — is broadly true and largely interchangeable across listings. What distinguishes one address from another is the lot itself, and lot literacy is the first place a careful buyer separates from a casual one.
   194	          </p>
   195	
   196	          <h3 className="mt-10 font-display text-2xl text-navy-800">Point lots — frontage on two waterways.</h3>
   197	          <p className="mt-3 text-[15px] leading-relaxed text-navy-800/85">
   198	            A point lot sits where two canals meet, with frontage on each side. Practically, that means the longest possible dockage envelope on a given parcel, the broadest water view in two directions, and meaningful resilience to a future neighbor changing what the opposing seawall looks like. Point lots typically trade at a premium for those exact reasons. They also tend to attract larger-vessel owners who value swing room — and in older parcel sections, the original 1960s point-lot footprints are still the most coveted.
   199	          </p>
   200	
   201	          <h3 className="mt-10 font-display text-2xl text-navy-800">Deepwater finger lots — single-canal frontage.</h3>
   202	          <p className="mt-3 text-[15px] leading-relaxed text-navy-800/85">
   203	            A finger lot has one waterway and one neighbor on each side. The dockage envelope is shorter than a point lot's, but the price point is more accessible for the same square footage of residence. Vessel beam matters here — a wide-beam yacht in a tight canal needs the turning radius confirmed before the offer, not after the survey. Finger lots also vary sharply by depth-at-low-tide; that single number rarely appears on the listing and is the one a buyer should ask Mia to verify against current bathymetry before writing.
   204	          </p>
   205	
   206	          <h3 className="mt-10 font-display text-2xl text-navy-800">Canal-wide lots — broad frontage with a wide opposite seawall.</h3>
   207	          <p className="mt-3 text-[15px] leading-relaxed text-navy-800/85">
   208	            Canal-wide lots sit on the wider waterways of Lighthouse Point — the parcels where the across-canal sightline is long enough to feel open. Dockage is competitive with a finger lot but the view profile is materially different: longer water vista, more privacy from the opposite bank, often softer wind exposure. Pricing tends to fall between point lots and standard finger lots and to track most closely with the perceived view quality.
   209	          </p>
   210	
   211	          <h3 className="mt-10 font-display text-2xl text-navy-800">Lakefront lots — interior water, indirect ocean route.</h3>
   212	          <p className="mt-3 text-[15px] leading-relaxed text-navy-800/85">
   213	            The interior lakes of Lighthouse Point produce visually striking parcels — broad water, no opposing seawall, a quieter aesthetic. The trade-off is the route: vessels still reach the Atlantic via the Hillsboro Inlet, but the path runs through interior canals first. For buyers who use a tender or smaller vessel and value the lake aesthetic, lakefront is a thoughtful choice. For buyers who plan to keep a primary cruising yacht, the directness of a canal-wide or point lot tends to be the more durable purchase.
   214	          </p>
   215	
   216	          <h3 className="mt-10 font-display text-2xl text-navy-800">What to verify on the address before the offer.</h3>
   217	          <p className="mt-3 text-[15px] leading-relaxed text-navy-800/85">
   218	            Three numbers, not categories: dockage dimensions (length, beam clearance, depth at mean low tide); ocean-route bridge clearances and timing to the Hillsboro Inlet for the specific vessel; and the seawall's age, material, and most recent inspection. The first two protect the lifestyle; the third protects the basis. Mia handles each of these as part of the standard pre-offer brief.
   219	          </p>
   220	
   221	          <div className="mt-10">
   222	            <Link
   223	              href="/markets/lighthouse-point/"
   224	              className="inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-full border border-navy-800/30 px-6 py-3 text-sm font-medium text-navy-800 transition-colors hover:border-brass-400 hover:text-brass-700"
   225	            >
   226	              Walk Lighthouse Point
   227	            </Link>
   228	            <Link
   229	              href="/contact/"
   230	              className="ml-3 inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-full bg-navy-800 px-6 py-3 text-sm font-medium text-cream-50 transition-colors hover:bg-navy-700"

   232	              Begin a Private Conversation
   233	            </Link>

   241	      />
   242	    </>

    54	function partitionMarkets(): {
    55	  primary: ReadonlyArray<Market>;
    56	  neighborhoods: ReadonlyArray<Market>;
    57	} {
    58	  const primary: Market[] = [];
    59	  const neighborhoods: Market[] = [];
    60	  for (const m of MARKETS) {
    61	    if (PRIMARY_SLUGS.has(m.slug)) primary.push(m);
    62	    else if (NEIGHBORHOOD_SLUGS.has(m.slug)) neighborhoods.push(m);
    63	    // Any future slug not in either set will not render here — surface as TS-build error
    64	    // instead by adding it to one of the sets above.
    65	  }
    66	  return { primary, neighborhoods };
    67	}

    19	
    20	/** Trim a string to <= maxLen characters, breaking at the last whitespace before maxLen and adding an ellipsis if cut. */
    21	function clampDescription(text: string, maxLen: number): string {
    22	  if (text.length <= maxLen) return text;
    23	  const slice = text.slice(0, maxLen);
    24	  const lastSpace = slice.lastIndexOf(" ");
    25	  const cut = lastSpace > 80 ? slice.slice(0, lastSpace) : slice;
    26	  return `${cut.replace(/[,.;:\s]+$/, "")}…`;
    27	}
    28	
    29	/** Build a 140-160 char metadata description from the market's first AEO sentence + Mia voice. */
    30	function buildMetaDescription(market: Market): string {
    31	  const firstSentenceMatch = market.aeoAnswer.match(/^(.+?[.!?])(\s|$)/);
    32	  const captured = firstSentenceMatch?.[1];
    33	  const lead = captured ? captured.trim() : market.aeoAnswer;
    34	  const tail = " Mia Sanabria, REALTOR® with LPT Realty.";
    35	  const target = 158;
    36	  const room = target - tail.length;
    37	  const trimmedLead = clampDescription(lead, room);
    38	  return `${trimmedLead}${tail}`;
    39	}
    40

    49	  const title = `${market.name} Luxury Real Estate | Mia Sanabria`;

   204	
   205	function Field({
   206	  label,
   207	  name,
   208	  type = "text",
   209	  autoComplete,
   210	  inputMode,
   211	  required = false,
   212	}: {
   213	  label: string;
   214	  name: string;
   215	  type?: string;
   216	  autoComplete?: string;
   217	  inputMode?: "email" | "tel" | "numeric";
   218	  required?: boolean;
   219	}) {
   220	  return (
   221	    <div>
   222	      <label htmlFor={name} className="block text-xs uppercase tracking-[0.25em] text-navy-800/70">
   223	        {label} {required ? <span aria-hidden>*</span> : null}
   224	      </label>
   225	      <input
   226	        id={name}
   227	        name={name}
   228	        type={type}
   229	        autoComplete={autoComplete}
   230	        inputMode={inputMode}
   231	        required={required}
   232	        aria-required={required}
   233	        className="mt-2 block w-full rounded-sm border border-navy-800/15 bg-cream-50 px-4 py-3 text-base text-navy-800 focus:border-brass-400 focus:outline-none"
   234	      />
   235	    </div>
   236	  );
   237	}
   238	
   239	function ContactRow({
   240	  Icon,
   241	  heading,
   242	  value,
   243	  hint,
   244	  href,
   245	}: {
   246	  Icon: React.ComponentType<{ className?: string; "aria-hidden"?: boolean }>;
   247	  heading: string;
   248	  value: string;
   249	  hint?: string;
   250	  href?: string;
   251	}) {
   252	  const inner = (
   253	    <div className="flex items-start gap-4">
   254	      <span className="mt-1 inline-flex h-10 w-10 flex-shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-navy-800 text-brass-200">
   255	        <Icon className="h-4 w-4" aria-hidden />
   256	      </span>
   257	      <div>
   258	        <div className="text-xs uppercase tracking-[0.3em] text-brass-700">{heading}</div>
   259	        <div className="mt-1 font-display text-lg text-navy-800">{value}</div>
   260	        {hint ? <div className="mt-1 text-sm text-navy-800/70">{hint}</div> : null}
   261	      </div>
   262	    </div>
   263	  );
   264	  return href ? (
   265	    <li>
   266	      <a href={href} className="block rounded-sm transition-colors hover:bg-cream-100">
   267	        {inner}
   268	      </a>
   269	    </li>
   270	  ) : (
   271	    <li>{inner}</li>
   272	  );
   273	}

   122	              </a>.

   124	
   125	            <fieldset className="mt-8">
   126	              <legend className="font-display text-sm tracking-[0.25em] text-brass-700">

   129	              <div className="mt-4 grid gap-5">
   130	                <Field label="Property Address" name="address" autoComplete="street-address" required />
   131	                <div className="grid gap-5 sm:grid-cols-3">

   140	                  >
   141	                    Additional Details or Upgrades
   142	                  </label>
   143	                  <textarea
   144	                    id="upgrades"
   145	                    name="upgrades"
   146	                    rows={4}
   147	                    className="mt-2 block w-full rounded-sm border border-navy-800/15 bg-cream-50 px-4 py-3 text-base text-navy-800 focus:border-brass-400 focus:outline-none"
   148	                    placeholder="Recent renovations, dock specifications, square footage, lot, view orientation, etc."
   149	                  />
   150	                </div>
   151	              </div>
   152	            </fieldset>

   163	                  name="email"

   168	                />

   170	              </div>
   171	            </fieldset>
   172	
   173	            <button
   174	              type="submit"
   175	              aria-describedby="valuation-form-helper"
   176	              className="mt-10 inline-flex w-full items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-full bg-navy-800 px-6 py-3 text-sm font-medium tracking-wide text-cream-50 transition-colors hover:bg-navy-700"
   177	            >
   178	              Request Valuation
   179	            </button>
   180	            <p id="valuation-form-helper" className="mt-3 text-xs text-navy-800/60">
   181	              This is a confidential request. By submitting, you agree to receive a private
   182	              response from Mia or her team at the email or phone number provided. Consent to
   183	              receive calls or text messages is not a condition of any service. We never share your
   184	              information. Standard message and data rates may apply.
   185	            </p>
   186	          </form>
   187	        </div>
   188	      </section>
   189	
   190	      <ValueProps
   191	        eyebrow="What the Valuation Includes"
   192	        heading="Sophisticated analysis, plain English."
   193	        items={[
   194	          {
   195	            heading: "Micro-market comparables",
   196	            body: "A pulled set of recent comparable transactions in your specific market — not a regional approximation.",
   197	          },
   198	          {
   199	            heading: "Brokerage relationship context",
   200	            body: "Where available, Mia's brokerage relationships add color from recent quietly-traded residences that public data feeds miss.",
   201	          },
   202	          {
   203	            heading: "Strategic positioning",
   204	            body: "Pricing recommendations grounded in the buyer pool the residence is actually competing for.",
   205	          },
   206	          {
   207	            heading: "Velocity analysis",
   208	            body: "Expected days-on-market, optimal listing window, and any seasonal considerations for your micro-market.",
   209	          },
   210	        ]}
   211	      />
   212	
   213	      <Faq items={VALUATION_FAQ} />
   214	    </>
   215	  );
   216	}
   217	
   218	function Field({
   219	  label,
   220	  name,
   221	  type = "text",
   222	  autoComplete,
   223	  inputMode,
   224	  required = false,
   225	}: {
   226	  label: string;
   227	  name: string;
   228	  type?: string;
   229	  autoComplete?: string;
   230	  inputMode?: "email" | "tel" | "numeric";
   231	  required?: boolean;
   232	}) {
   233	  return (
   234	    <div>
   235	      <label htmlFor={name} className="block text-xs uppercase tracking-[0.25em] text-navy-800/70">
   236	        {label} {required ? <span aria-hidden>*</span> : null}
   237	      </label>
   238	      <input
   239	        id={name}
   240	        name={name}
   241	        type={type}
   242	        autoComplete={autoComplete}
   243	        inputMode={inputMode}
   244	        required={required}
   245	        aria-required={required}
   246	        className="mt-2 block w-full rounded-sm border border-navy-800/15 bg-cream-50 px-4 py-3 text-base text-navy-800 focus:border-brass-400 focus:outline-none"
   247	      />
   248	    </div>
   249	  );
   250	}
